# KTM 85 SX 19/16 2027: aftermarket parts

3631 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 191 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1224 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2216 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
